                 ---------------------------------------------

    THE FIRST TWO DIGITS OF THIS VARIABLE IDENTIFY THE STATE OF
    RESIDENCE (SEE REF.NO. 3005 FOR THE STATE CODES); THE LAST
    TWO DIGITS OF THIS VARIABLE CONTAIN THE ACTUAL CONGRESSIONAL
    DISTRICT NUMBER.

VAR 763010    NAME-PSU CODE
              COLUMNS 36   - 38
              NUMERIC
              MD=0

                 PRIMARY SAMPLING UNIT (PSU) OF INTERVIEW
                 ----------------------------------------

                 THE FIRST 2 DIGITS OF THIS VARIABLE IDENTIFY THE PRIMARY
                 SAMPLING UNIT.  THE NEW YORK CITY AND CHICAGO PSU'S ARE
                 EXCEPTIONS IN THAT THEY ARE IDENTIFIED BY TWO OR MORE
                 2-DIGIT CODES.  THE CHICAGO PSU IS IDENTIFIED BY CODES
                 '10' AND '11'; THE NEW YORK CITY PSU IS IDENTIFIED BY THE
                 CODE RANGE '20'-'24'.  IN ADDITION, THE FIRST DIGIT OF THE
                 PSU IDENTIFIES THE MAJOR GEOGRAPHIC REGION.

                                SELF-REPRESENTING     NON-SELF-REPRESENTING
                 REGION         PSU'S - 1ST DIGIT       PSU'S - 1ST DIGIT
                 ------         -----------------     --------------------

                 WEST                   0                       8
                 NORTH CENTRAL          1                      6,7
                 NORTHEAST              2                       9
                 SOUTH                  3                     3,4,5

                 THE THIRD DIGIT OF THE PSU IS BASED ON THE POPULATION
                 DENSITY OR DEGREE OF URBANIZATION.

                 3RD DIGIT   DEGREE OF URBANIZATION
                 ---------   ----------------------

                   1,2       CENTRAL CITIES OF THE 12 LARGEST SMSA'S (SELF-
                              REPRESENTING PSU'S)
                   3,4       SUBURBS OF THE SELF-REPRESENTING PSU'S
                   5,6       NON-SELF-REPRESENTING PSU'S WHICH DO INCLUDE
                              A CENTRAL CITY OF A SMSA
                   7,8       NON-SELF REPRESENTING PSU'S WHICH DO NOT INCLUDE
                              A CENTRAL CITY OF A SMSA

                 SEE APPENDIX NOTE

==============================
VAR 763011    NAME-DEGREE OF URBANIZATION
              COLUMNS 39   - 39

                 DEGREE OF URBANIZATION OF PSU

                              REPRESENTING PSU'S)
                   3,4       SUBURBS OF THE SELF-REPRESENTING PSU'S
                   5,6       NON-SELF-REPRESENTING PSU'S WHICH DO INCLUDE
                              A CENTRAL CITY OF A SMSA
                   7,8       NON-SELF REPRESENTING PSU'S WHICH DO NOT INCLUDE
                              A CENTRAL CITY OF A SMSA

VAR 763205    NAME-R'S ATTITUDE RE BUSING#1
              COLUMNS 398  - 399
              NUMERIC
              MD=GE 98

                 FIRST RESPONSE TO BUSING FOR SCHOOL INTEGRATION

                 NOW I'D LIKE TO ASK YOU SOME QUESTIONS ABOUT RELATIONS
                 BETWEEN THE RACES.

                 Q.E1(1).  LET'S START WITH THE ISSUE OF BUSING.  THE COURTS
                 HAVE ORDERED BUSING FOR SOME SCHOOL CHILDREN IN SEVERAL
                 CITIES IN THE UNITED STATES.  WHAT DO YOU THINK OF BUSING
                 AS A WAY TO INTEGRATE THE SCHOOLS?  (PROBE:  CAN YOU TELL
                 ME WHY YOU FEEL THAT WAY?)

                 -----------------------------------------------------------

                 PRO-BUSING MENTIONS

                 55  02.  EQUAL OPPORTUNITY -- PROVIDES FOR EQUAL OPPOR-
                          TUNITY; WE SHOULD ALL HAVE EQUAL OPPORTUNITY; ALL
                          SCHOOLS SHOULD BE THE SAME/EQUAL
                135  04.  PRO-INTEGRATION; ONLY WAY TO ACHIEVE INTEGRATION
                 17  05.  INCREASES THE QUALITY OF EDUCATION FOR BLACKS/
                          WHITES
                 37  10.  PROMOTES INTERRACIAL UNDERSTANDING; INCREASES UN-
                          DERSTANDING; WE MUST GET TOGETHER/WORK TOGETHER
                          TOWARD SOLVING OUR PROBLEMS
                  3  12.  PROMOTES INDIVIDUAL GROWTH; IT MAKES KIDS MORE IN-
                          DEPENDENT TO BE AT SCHOOL WHERE THEY DON'T KNOW
                          ANYONE
                  1  15.  KEEPS DOWN CONFLICT; KEEPS KIDS OUT OF TROUBLE;
                          DOESN'T CAUSE THAT MUCH TROUBLE
                  4  17.  SOLVE PROBLEMS -- NA WHAT, NOT FURTHER SPECIFIED
                 22  29.  OTHER PRO-BUSING RESPONSES
                 95  49.  IT'S OK; FINE; A GOOD THING -- NA WHY OR HOW

                 NEUTRAL MENTIONS

                 29  31.  FOR INTEGRATION/NOT AGAINST BLACKS -- STAND ON
                          BUSING NA
                  6  32.  DEPENDS ON AGE OF CHILD; IT'S ALL RIGHT IF CHILD IS
                          OLD ENOUGH; SHOULDN'T BE FOR VERY YOUNG; SHOULD BE
                          ONLY FOR OLDER KIDS
                 11  33.  IT'S THE LAW AND SHOULD BE OBEYED
                 32  34.  REFERENCES TO BUSING BEING ALL RIGHT IN SOME AREAS/
                          COMMUNITIES BUT NOT OTHERS -- NOT CLEARLY OPPOSED
                          TO BUSING:  "DEPENDS ON THE PARTICULAR COMMUNITY"
                 11  35.  CONDITIONAL APPROVAL OF BUSING IF IT WILL IMPROVE
                          OR MAINTAIN QUALITY OF EDUCATION/RACIAL SITUATION
                 33  39.  OTHER NEUTRAL MENTION

                 ANTI-BUSING MENTIONS

                  2  52.  SPECIAL TREATMENT FOR BLACKS; BLACKS SHOULDN'T BE
                          GIVEN THINGS; BLACKS SHOULD HAVE TO WORK HARD TOO;
                          WE WORKED FOR EDUCATIONAL OPPORTUNITIES BLACKS
                          SHOULD ALSO
                 84  54.  PRO-SEGREGATION -- KEEP WHITES AND BLACKS SEPARATE;
                          DON'T BELIEVE IN MIXING THE SCHOOLS; BLACKS SHOULD
                          STAY IN THE SAME SCHOOLS WHERE THEY ARE AMONG THEIR
                          OWN KIND
                173  56.  AGAINST COMPULSORY BUSING; SHOULD BE VOLUNTARY;
                          AGAINST FORCED BUSING; INFRINGES ON OUR RIGHTS TO
                          CHOOSE WHERE CHILDREN SHALL GO TO SCHOOL
                 34  60.  REDUCES QUALITY OF EDUCATION; DETRIMENTAL TO LEARN-
                          ING; KIDS CAN'T STUDY WITH ALL THE FUSS OVER BUS-
                          ING; KIDS CAN'T STUDY BECAUSE BUS RIDE TIRES THEM
                          OUT
                  8  61.  DOESN'T IMPROVE QUALITY OF EDUCATION FOR BLACKS
                109  62.  DETRIMENTAL TO CHILDREN PSYCHOLOGICALLY, MAKES THEM
                          FEEL UNCERTAIN; IT'S NOT BENEFITTING THE KIDS -- NA
                          WHETHER EDUCATION OR PSYCHOLOGICAL DETRIMENT
                 27  63.  DOESN'T HELP BLACKS/BLACK KIDS -- PSYCHOLOGICALLY
                          OR NA HOW; BLACK PEOPLE DON'T WANT IT EITHER
                 77  64.  DETRIMENTAL TO INTER-GROUP HARMONY:  TENDS TO IN-
                          FLAME THE PUBLIC, MAKES PEOPLE REBEL; IT CAUSES
                          HARD FEELINGS, FORCES PEOPLE TO DO THINGS THAT ARE
                          NOT RIGHT; PEOPLE ARE NOT HAPPY
                 33  66.  SAFETY REASONS:  BUSING IS DANGEROUS; LEADS TO
                          RIOTS; I AM AFRAID/WORRIED FOR MY CHILD; BUSING
                          INCREASES CRIME AND DISORDER IN SCHOOLS
                  5  67.  DOESN'T HELP WHITE (WHITE NEEDS); "WHITES DO NOT
                          WANT TO BE BUSED"

                 PREFER ALTERNATIVE SOLUTION

                 98  72.  NEED BETTER SCHOOLS, MATERIALS OR TEACHERS:  IM-
                          PROVE ALL SCHOOLS INSTEAD OF BUSING; IMPROVE EDUCA-
                          TION IN ALL SCHOOLS; GIVE ALL SCHOOLS EQUAL MONEY,
                          MATERIALS, ETC.
                 44  74.  INTEGRATE THE NEIGHBORHOODS/HOUSING INSTEAD OF
                          BUSING
                 10  76.  CHANGE/EDUCATE ADULTS, NOT CHILDREN; PROBLEM IS
                          WITH PARENTS, NOT CHILDREN; LEARN ONE THING IN THE
                          HOME, A DIFFERENT THING IN SCHOOL
                268  79.  NA WHAT ALTERNATIVE, BUT BUSING IS NOT THE SOLUTION
                          TO BETTER EDUCATION; OTHER ALTERNATIVE SOLUTIONS,
                          NA WHAT OR NOT CODEABLE; "FOR INTEGRATION BUT NOT
                          IN FAVOR OF BUSING"; BUSING JUST WON'T WORK (NA
                          WHY); NOT GOING TO/HASN'T SOLVE(D) ANYTHING WITH
                          BUSING
                230  80.  ECONOMIC REASONS, GENERAL:  WASTE OF TAXPAYERS
                          MONEY; WASTES MONEY, RAISES TAXES, COULD USE THE
                          MONEY MORE WISELY, WASTES GASOLINE
                 19  81.  TAX SUPPORT TO SPECIAL SCHOOL SYSTEM; WE PAY TAXES
                          SO CHILD CAN GO HERE
                 36  82.  INCONVENIENCE; CHILD/PARENT CAN'T PARTICIPATE IN
                          (AFTER) SCHOOL ACTIVITIES; DIFFICULT TO GET CHILD
                          FROM SCHOOL (INCLUDING IF SICK OR HURT AT SCHOOL),
                          WASTES TIME
                512  84.  PROXIMITY/PREFER NEIGHBORHOOD SCHOOL, NA WHY:  WE
                          LIVE NEAR A SCHOOL AND WANT OUR CHILD TO GO HERE;
                          WANT SCHOOL CLOSE TO HOME; DON'T WANT CHILD GOING
                          AWAY ACROSS TOWN
                  6  85.  IT'S THE LAW, BUT I DON'T LIKE IT
                 44  90.  JUST CREATES MORE PROBLEMS/TROUBLE (NA WHAT)
                344  91.  I JUST HATE IT, NA WHY; IT'S JUST NOT A GOOD THING/
                          DON'T LIKE IT
                 28  97.  OTHER ANTI-BUSING MENTIONS

                144  98.  DK
                 53  99.  NA

                 Q.G1.  AS YOU KNOW, MANY PEOPLE WERE MENTIONED THIS YEAR AS
                 POSSIBLE CANDIDATES FOR PRESIDENT OR VICE-PRESIDENT BY THE
                 POLITICAL PARTIES.  WE WOULD LIKE TO GET YOUR FEELINGS
                 TOWARD SOME OF THESE PEOPLE.

                 THAT PERSON WITH WHAT WE CALL A FEELING THERMOMETER (INTER-
                 VIEWER:  SHOW RESPONDENT BOOKLET, PAGE 12).  RATINGS BETWEEN
                 50 AND 100 DEGREES MEAN THAT YOU FEEL FAVORABLY AND WARM
                 TOWARD THE PERSON, RATINGS BETWEEN 0 AND 50 DEGREES MEAN
                 THAT YOU DON'T FEEL FAVORABLY TOWARDS THE PERSON AND THAT
                 YOU DON'T CARE TOO MUCH FOR THAT PERSON.  IF YOU DON'T FEEL
                 PARTICULARLY WARM OR COLD TOWARD A PERSON YOU WOULD RATE
                 THEM AT 50 DEGREES.  IF WE COME TO A PERSON YOU DON'T KNOW
                 MUCH ABOUT, JUST TELL ME AND WE'LL MOVE ON TO THE NEXT ONE.

                 HIM USING THE THERMOMETER?

VAR 763409    NAME-R'S EMPLOYMENT STATUS
              COLUMNS 659  - 659
              NUMERIC
              MD=GE 9

                 Q.S6.  WE'D LIKE TO KNOW IF YOU ARE WORKING NOW, <TEMPOR-
                 ARILY LAID OFF>, OR ARE YOU UNEMPLOYED, RETIRED, <PERMANENTLY
                 DISABLED>, (A HOUSEWIFE), (A STUDENT) OR WHAT?

                 ------------------------------------------------------------

           1559  1.  WORKING NOW
             46  2.  TEMPORARILY LAID OFF
            124  4.  UNEMPLOYED
            374  5.  RETIRED
             76  6.  PERMANENTLY DISABLED
            611  7.  HOUSEWIFE (INCLUDING HOUSEWIVES WHO WORK LESS THAN
                     20 HOURS/WEEK)
             77  8.  STUDENT (INCLUDING STUDENTS WHO WORK LESS THAN 20
                     HOURS/WEEK)

              3  9.  NA

          CODING PROCEDURE:  IF THE RESPONDENT IS RETIRED OR DISABLED
          BUT IS CURRENTLY WORKING IN A JOB RELATED TO HIS/HER PRIOR
          OCCUPATION, HE/SHE IS CODED '1' IN THIS VARIABLE AND TREATED
          AS "WORKING NOW" IN THE REST OF THE RESPONDENT'S OCCUPATION
          SECTION VARIABLES; NO DATA ARE CODED FOR THE JOB PRIOR TO
          THE CURRENT ONE.  IF THE RESPONDENT IS RETIRED OR DISABLED
          AND NOT WORKING OR CURRENTLY WORKING IN A JOB UNRELATED TO
          HIS/HER PRIOR OCCUPATION, HE/SHE IS CODED '5' OR '6' IN THIS
          VARIABLE AND TREATED AS "RETIRED" OR "DISABLED" IN THE REST
          OF THE RESPONDENT'S OCCUPATION SECTION VARIABLES; DATA ARE
          GATHERED FOR THE JOB PRIOR TO RETIREMENT OR DISABLEMENT.

VAR 763431    NAME-F HD EMPLT STATUS
              COLUMNS 700  - 700
              NUMERIC
              MD=GE 9

                 FAMILY HEAD'S EMPLOYMENT STATUS

                 Q.S20(1).  WE'D ALSO LIKE TO KNOW ABOUT THE HEAD OF THE
                 FAMILY.  IS (HE/SHE) WORKING NOW, <TEMPORARILY LAID OFF>,
                 UNEMPLOYED, RETIRED, <PERMANENTLY DISABLED>, (A HOUSEWIFE),
                 (A STUDENT), OR WHAT?

                 -----------------------------------------------------------

                 IF THE RESPONDENT IS THE FAMILY HEAD, HIS/HER OCCUPATIONAL
                 DATA ARE ALSO CODED IN THIS VARIABLE.

               1939  1.  WORKING NOW
                 55  2.  TEMPORARILY LAID OFF
                111  4.  UNEMPLOYED
                454  5.  RETIRED
                101  6.  PERMANENTLY DISABLED
                161  7.  HOUSEWIFE (INCLUDING HOUSEWIVES WHO WORK LESS THAN
                         20 HOURS/WEEK)
                 39  8.  STUDENT (INCLUDING STUDENTS WHO WORK LESS THAN
                         20 HOURS/WEEK)

                 11  9.  NA

                 CODING PROCEDURE:  IF THE FAMILY HEAD IS RETIRED OR DISABLED
                 BUT IS CURRENTLY WORKING IN A JOB RELATED TO HIS/HER PRIOR
                 OCCUPATION, HE/SHE IS CODED '1' IN THIS VARIABLE AND TREATED
                 AS "WORKING NOW" IN THE REST OF THE FAMILY HEAD'S OCCUPATION
                 SECTION VARIABLES; NO DATA ARE CODED FOR THE JOB PRIOR TO
                 THE CURRENT ONE.  IF THE FAMILY HEAD IS RETIRED OR DISABLED
                 AND NOT WORKING OR CURRENTLY WORKING IN A JOB UNRELATED TO
                 HIS/HER PRIOR OCCUPATION, HE/SHE IS CODED '5' OR '6' IN THIS
                 VARIABLE AND TREATED AS "RETIRED" OR "DISABLED" IN THE REST
                 OF THE FAMILY HEAD'S OCCUPATION SECTION VARIABLES; DATA ARE
                 GATHERED FOR THE JOB PRIOR TO RETIREMENT OR DISABLEMENT.

VAR 763451    NAME-WIFE EMPLT STATUS
              COLUMNS 739  - 739
              NUMERIC
              MD=0 OR GE 9

                 WIFE OF HEAD'S EMPLOYMENT STATUS

                 Q.S20(2).  WE'D ALSO LIKE TO KNOW ABOUT THE WIFE OF THE
                 FAMILY.  IS SHE WORKING NOW, <TEMPORARILY LAID OFF>, UN-
                 EMPLOYED, RETIRED, <PERMANENTLY DISABLED>, (A HOUSEWIFE),
                 (A STUDENT), OR WHAT?
                 ---------------------------------------------------------

                 IF RESPONDENT IS THE WIFE OF FAMILY HEAD, HER OCCUPATIONAL
                 DATA ARE ALSO CODED IN THIS VARIABLE.

                756  1.  WORKING NOW
                  9  2.  TEMPORARILY LAID OFF
                 46  4.  UNEMPLOYED
                 79  5.  RETIRED
                 12  6.  PERMANENTLY DISABLED
                849  7.  HOUSEWIFE (INCLUDING HOUSEWIVES WHO WORK LESS THAN
                         20 HOURS/WEEK)
                 17  8.  STUDENT (INCLUDING STUDENTS WHO WORK LESS THAN
                         20 HOURS/WEEK)

                  2  9.  NA
               1102  0.  INAP., CODED 1 IN INTERVIEWER CHECKPOINT D; OR CODED
                         3 IN INTERVIEWER CHECKPOINT D AND R IS NOT WIFE OF
                         HEAD

                 CODING PROCEDURE:  IF THE WIFE OF FAMILY HEAD IS RETIRED OR
                 DISABLED BUT IS CURRENTLY WORKING IN A JOB RELATED TO HER
                 PRIOR OCCUPATION, SHE IS CODED '1' IN THIS VARIABLE AND
                 TREATED AS "WORKING NOW" IN THE REST OF THE WIFE OF HEAD'S
                 OCCUPATION SECTION VARIABLES; NO DATA ARE CODED FOR THE JOB
                 PRIOR TO THE CURRENT ONE.  IF THE WIFE OF FAMILY HEAD IS RE-
                 TIRED OR DISABLED AND NOT WORKING OR CURRENTLY WORKING IN A
                 JOB UNRELATED TO HER PRIOR OCCUPATION, SHE IS CODED '5' OR
                 '6' IN THIS VARIABLE AND TREATED AS "RETIRED" OR "DISABLED"
                 IN THE REST OF THE WIFE OF HEAD'S OCCUPATION SECTION VARI-
                 ABLES; DATA ARE GATHERED FOR THE JOB PRIOR TO RETIREMENT OR
                 DISABLEMENT.

VAR 763634    NAME-DEBATE INDEX
              COLUMNS 1006 - 1006
              NUMERIC
              MD=0 OR GE 9

                 DEBATE CANDIDATE COMPARISON CODE
                 --------------------------------

                 SUMMARIZATION OF R'S STATEMENTS IN Q.C8A-Q.C11

                204  1.  EXPLICIT COMPARISON MADE BETWEEN FORD AND CARTER.
                         THIS MAY INCLUDE PRONOUNS, BUT THE COMPARISON MUST
                         HAVE BEEN MADE EXPLICITLY BETWEEN THE CANDIDATES.
                         COMMENTS WHICH APPLY EQUALLY TO BOTH CANDIDATES
                         ("BOTH BETTER ON THEIR FEET THAN I EXPECTED," "BOTH
                         VERY TIRED AND BOTH BRIGHT") ARE NOT CODED HERE.
                 15  2.  EXPLICIT COMPARISON MADE BETWEEN DOLE AND MONDALE.
                112  3.  IMPLICIT COMPARISON MADE BETWEEN FORD AND CARTER.
                         SUCH COMMENTS AS "MORE SINCERE," OR "MORE RELAXED"
                         ARE CODED HERE.  COMPARISONS WITH AN ABSTRACT STAN-
                         DARD, "SMILED TOO MUCH," OR "BETTER THAN I EXPECTED"
                         DO NOT GO HERE.
                  4  4.  IMPLICIT COMPARISON MADE BETWEEN DOLE AND MONDALE.

                 --IF RESPONSE TO Q.D3 WAS "YES"--

                 Q.D3A.  WHO DID YOU VOTE FOR <FOR U.S. SENATOR>?

                 Q.D3B.  WHICH PARTY IS THAT?
                 ------------------------------------------------

                 R'S MENTION OF CANDIDATE NAME WAS CODED FROM THE LIST ONLY
                 IF THAT INDIVIDUAL ACTUALLY RAN FOR SENATOR IN R'S STATE AND
                 IF R CORRECTLY IDENTIFIED THE CANDIDATE'S PARTY.  IF THE
                 NAME MENTIONED BY R WAS NOT IN THE LIST OF STATE CANDIDATES,
                 OR WAS ASSOCIATED WITH THE WRONG PARTY, "995" WAS CODED.
                 FOR EXAMPLE, IF "HAYAKAWA, THE DEMOCRATIC CANDIDATE" WAS
                 MENTIONED BY R FOR CALIFORNIA, THEN "995" WAS CODED.

                 SEE APPENDIX NOTE

                     998.  DK NAME OF CANDIDATE
                     999.  NA; REFUSED TO SAY
                     000.  INAP., CODED 5-9 OR 0 IN Q.D3; NO POST ELECTION
                           INTERVIEW

                 Q.G8.  (INTERVIEWER:  SHOW RESPONDENT BOOKLET, PAGE 16) AS
